    71	template <typename T>
    72	class DeviceBuffer {
    73	public:
    74	    DeviceBuffer() = default;
    75	
    76	    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
    77	        allocate(count);
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    ~DeviceBuffer() {
    81	        if (data_ != nullptr) {
    82	            cudaFree(data_);
    83	        }
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    87	    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    88	
    89	    DeviceBuffer(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept
    90	        : data_(std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr)) {}
    91	
    92	    DeviceBuffer& operator=(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept {
    93	        if (this != &other) {
    94	            if (data_ != nullptr) {
    95	                cudaFree(data_);
    96	            }
    97	            data_ = std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr);
    98	        }
    99	        return *this;
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    void allocate(size_t count) {
   103	        if (count != 0) {
   104	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
   105	                                  count * sizeof(T)));
   106	        }
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    T* get() { return data_; }
   110	    const T* get() const { return data_; }
   111	
   112	private:
   113	    T* data_ = nullptr;
   114	};
   115	
   116	// Material type IDs
   117	constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;
   118	constexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;
   119	constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;
   120	
   121	// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh
   122	// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries
   123	void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {
   124	    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;
   125	    
   126	    // Initialize materials
   127	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material
   128	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material
   129	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material
   130	    
   131	    // Allocate elements
   132	    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);
   133	    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);
   134	    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);
   135	    
   136	    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy
   137	    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
   138	        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
   139	        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;
   140	        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;
   141	        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;
   142	    }
   143	    
   144	    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid
   145	    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {
   146	        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {
   147	            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;
   148	            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];
   149	            
   150	            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)
   151	            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
   152	            
   153	            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {
   154	                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];
   155	                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];
   156	                
   157	                // Check if neighbor is within bounds
   158	                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {
   159	                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;
   160	                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;
   161	                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;
   162	                    elem.num_connections++;
   163	                }
   164	            }
   165	        }
   166	    }
   167	    
   168	    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
   169	    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
   170	    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
   171	    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
   172	    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
   173	    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
   174	}
   175	
   176	// Materials are tiny and are read uniformly by almost every warp. Constant
   177	// memory avoids a global-memory indirection without specializing the mesh.
   178	__constant__ Material device_materials[MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS];
   179	
   180	// Connectivity is stored slot-major on the device: all first neighbors are
   181	// contiguous, then all second neighbors, and so on. This preserves each
   182	// element's original neighbor order while making warp accesses coalesced.
   183	template <unsigned MaxDegree>
   184	__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
   185	void simulationIteration(
   186	    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
   187	    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
   188	    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
   189	    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_fluxes,
   190	    const val_t* __restrict__ current_energy,
   191	    const val_t* __restrict__ current_accumulated_flux,
   192	    val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
   193	    val_t* __restrict__ next_accumulated_flux,
   194	    size_t element_count) {
   195	    const size_t element_idx =
   196	        static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   197	    if (element_idx >= element_count) {
   198	        return;
   199	    }
   200	
   201	    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[element_idx]];
   202	    const val_t energy = current_energy[element_idx];
   203	    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
   204	    const unsigned connection_count = connection_counts[element_idx];
   205	
   206	    if constexpr (MaxDegree > 0) {
   207	#pragma unroll
   208	        for (unsigned connection = 0; connection < MaxDegree; ++connection) {
   209	            if (connection < connection_count) {
   210	                const size_t connection_offset =
   211	                    static_cast<size_t>(connection) * element_count +
   212	                    element_idx;
   213	                const uint32_t neighbor_idx =
   214	                    connected_indices[connection_offset];
   215	                total_flux += (current_energy[neighbor_idx] - energy) *
   216	                              material.transfer_coeff *
   217	                              connected_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
   218	            }
   219	        }
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    next_energy[element_idx] = energy + total_flux;
   223	    next_accumulated_flux[element_idx] =
   224	        current_accumulated_flux[element_idx] + fabs(total_flux);
   225	}
   226	
   227	class GpuWorld {
   228	public:
   229	    GpuWorld(const World& world, int n_iters)
   230	        : element_count_(world.elements_static.size()) {
   231	        if (element_count_ > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
   232	            std::fprintf(stderr,
   233	                         "Mesh has too many elements for GPU connectivity\n");
   234	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   235	        }
   236	        if (world.materials.empty() ||
   237	            world.materials.size() > MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS) {
   238	            std::fprintf(stderr, "GPU supports between 1 and %d materials\n",
   239	                         MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS);
   240	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   241	        }
   242	        if (world.elements_dynamic.size() != element_count_) {
   243	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Static and dynamic mesh sizes differ\n");
   244	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   245	        }
   246	
   247	        std::vector<uint8_t> host_material_indices(element_count_);
   248	        std::vector<uint8_t> host_connection_counts(element_count_);
   249	        std::vector<val_t> host_energy(element_count_);
   250	        std::vector<val_t> host_accumulated_flux(element_count_);
   251	
   252	        for (size_t i = 0; i < element_count_; ++i) {
   253	            const ElementStatic& element = world.elements_static[i];
   254	            if (element.material_idx >= world.materials.size() ||
   255	                element.num_connections > MAX_CONNECTIONS) {
   256	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Invalid mesh metadata at element %zu\n", i);
   257	                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   258	            }
   259	            host_material_indices[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.material_idx);
   260	            host_connection_counts[i] =
   261	                static_cast<uint8_t>(element.num_connections);
   262	            max_degree_ = std::max(
   263	                max_degree_, static_cast<unsigned>(element.num_connections));
   264	            host_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;
   265	            host_accumulated_flux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;
   266	        }
   267	
   268	        std::vector<uint32_t> host_connected_indices(
   269	            element_count_ * max_degree_);
   270	        std::vector<val_t> host_connected_fluxes(element_count_ * max_degree_);
   271	        for (size_t i = 0; i < element_count_; ++i) {
   272	            const ElementStatic& element = world.elements_static[i];
   273	            for (unsigned connection = 0;
   274	                 connection < element.num_connections; ++connection) {
   275	                if (element.connected_idx[connection] >= element_count_) {
   276	                    std::fprintf(stderr,
   277	                                 "Invalid neighbor at element %zu, slot %u\n",
   278	                                 i, connection);
   279	                    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   280	                }
   281	                const size_t offset =
   282	                    static_cast<size_t>(connection) * element_count_ + i;
   283	                host_connected_indices[offset] =
   284	                    static_cast<uint32_t>(element.connected_idx[connection]);
   285	                host_connected_fluxes[offset] =
   286	                    element.connected_flux[connection];
   287	            }
   288	        }
   289	
   290	        material_indices_.allocate(element_count_);
   291	        connection_counts_.allocate(element_count_);
   292	        connected_indices_.allocate(host_connected_indices.size());
   293	        connected_fluxes_.allocate(host_connected_fluxes.size());
   294	        energy_[0].allocate(element_count_);
   295	        energy_[1].allocate(element_count_);
   296	        accumulated_flux_[0].allocate(element_count_);
   297	        accumulated_flux_[1].allocate(element_count_);
   298	
   299	        copyToDevice(material_indices_, host_material_indices);
   300	        copyToDevice(connection_counts_, host_connection_counts);
   301	        copyToDevice(connected_indices_, host_connected_indices);
   302	        copyToDevice(connected_fluxes_, host_connected_fluxes);
   303	        copyToDevice(energy_[0], host_energy);
   304	        copyToDevice(accumulated_flux_[0], host_accumulated_flux);
   305	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(
   306	            device_materials, world.materials.data(),
   307	            world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
   308	
   309	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   310	        prepareGraph(n_iters);
   311	    }
   312	
   313	    ~GpuWorld() {
   314	        if (batch_graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
   315	            cudaGraphExecDestroy(batch_graph_exec_);
   316	        }
   317	        if (remainder_graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
   318	            cudaGraphExecDestroy(remainder_graph_exec_);
   319	        }
   320	        if (stream_ != nullptr) {
   321	            cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
   322	        }
   323	    }
   324	
   325	    GpuWorld(const GpuWorld&) = delete;
   326	    GpuWorld& operator=(const GpuWorld&) = delete;
   327	
   328	    void run() {
   329	        for (int batch = 0; batch < graph_batch_count_; ++batch) {
   330	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(batch_graph_exec_, stream_));
   331	        }
   332	        if (remainder_graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
   333	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(remainder_graph_exec_, stream_));
   334	        }
   335	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    void download(World& world) const {
   339	        std::vector<val_t> host_energy(element_count_);
   340	        std::vector<val_t> host_accumulated_flux(element_count_);
   341	        const size_t byte_count = element_count_ * sizeof(val_t);
   342	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_energy.data(), energy_[active_buffer_].get(),
   343	                              byte_count, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   344	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_accumulated_flux.data(),
   345	                              accumulated_flux_[active_buffer_].get(),
   346	                              byte_count, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   347	        for (size_t i = 0; i < element_count_; ++i) {
   348	            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = host_energy[i];
   349	            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = host_accumulated_flux[i];
   350	        }
   351	    }
   352	
   353	private:
   354	    template <typename T>
   355	    static void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination,
   356	                             const std::vector<T>& source) {
   357	        if (!source.empty()) {
   358	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(),
   359	                                  source.size() * sizeof(T),
   360	                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   361	        }
   362	    }
   363	
   364	    template <unsigned MaxDegree>
   365	    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
   366	        const unsigned write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1U;
   367	        const int block_count = static_cast<int>(
   368	            (element_count_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
   369	        simulationIteration<MaxDegree><<<block_count, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0,
   370	                                         stream_>>>(
   371	            material_indices_.get(), connection_counts_.get(),
   372	            connected_indices_.get(), connected_fluxes_.get(),
   373	            energy_[read_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[read_buffer].get(),
   374	            energy_[write_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[write_buffer].get(),
   375	            element_count_);
   376	    }
   377	
   378	    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
   379	        switch (max_degree_) {
   380	            case 0: launchIteration<0>(read_buffer); break;
   381	            case 1: launchIteration<1>(read_buffer); break;
   382	            case 2: launchIteration<2>(read_buffer); break;
   383	            case 3: launchIteration<3>(read_buffer); break;
   384	            case 4: launchIteration<4>(read_buffer); break;
   385	            case 5: launchIteration<5>(read_buffer); break;
   386	            case 6: launchIteration<6>(read_buffer); break;
   387	            case 7: launchIteration<7>(read_buffer); break;
   388	            case 8: launchIteration<8>(read_buffer); break;
   389	            default:
   390	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported GPU mesh degree: %u\n",
   391	                             max_degree_);
   392	                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   393	        }
   394	    }
   395	
   396	    void prepareGraph(int n_iters) {
   397	        active_buffer_ = static_cast<unsigned>(n_iters) & 1U;
   398	        graph_batch_count_ = n_iters / CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE;
   399	        if (graph_batch_count_ != 0) {
   400	            batch_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
   401	        }
   402	        remainder_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(n_iters % CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
   403	    }
   404	
   405	    cudaGraphExec_t makeGraph(int iteration_count) {
   406	        if (iteration_count == 0) {
   407	            return nullptr;
   408	        }
   409	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_,
   410	                                          cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
   411	        for (int iter = 0; iter < iteration_count; ++iter) {
   412	            launchIteration(static_cast<unsigned>(iter) & 1U);
   413	        }
   414	
   415	        cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
   416	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph));
   417	        cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec = nullptr;
   418	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   419	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
   420	        return graph_exec;
   421	    }
   422	
   423	    size_t element_count_ = 0;
   424	    unsigned max_degree_ = 0;
   425	    unsigned active_buffer_ = 0;
   426	    int graph_batch_count_ = 0;
   427	    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
   428	    cudaGraphExec_t batch_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
   429	    cudaGraphExec_t remainder_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
   430	    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> material_indices_;
   431	    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> connection_counts_;
   432	    DeviceBuffer<uint32_t> connected_indices_;
   433	    DeviceBuffer<val_t> connected_fluxes_;
   434	    DeviceBuffer<val_t> energy_[2];
   435	    DeviceBuffer<val_t> accumulated_flux_[2];
   436	};
   437	
   438	// Validate simulation results
   439	bool validateResults(const World& world) {
   440	    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
   441	    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
   442	    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   443	    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   444	    
   445	    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
   446	        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;
   447	        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;
   448	        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);
   449	        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);
   450	    }
   451	    
   452	    printf("Validation results:\n");
   453	    printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
   454	    printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
   455	    printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
   456	    
   457	    // Check for numerical issues
   458	    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
   459	    
   460	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
   460	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
   461	        printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
   462	        return false;
   463	    }
   464	    
   465	    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
   466	        printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
   467	        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows
   468	    }
   469	    
   470	    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
   471	        printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
   472	        return false;
   473	    }
   474	    
   475	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
   476	        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
   477	        return false;
   478	    }
   479	    
   480	    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   481	    return true;
   482	}
   483	
   484	// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
   485	uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
   486	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   487	    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
   488	        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
   489	        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);
   490	        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);
   491	        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   492	        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
   493	    }
   494	    return hash;
   495	}
   496	
   497	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   498	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   499	    printf("Options:\n");
   500	    printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
   501	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
   502	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   503	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   504	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   505	}
   506	
   507	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   508	    int n_elems_root = 512;
   509	    int n_iters = 10;
   510	    bool validate = false;
   511	    bool printResults = false;
   512	    
   513	    // Parse command line arguments
   514	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   515	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   516	            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);
   517	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   518	            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);
   519	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   520	            validate = true;
   521	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   522	            printResults = true;
   523	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   524	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   525	            return 0;
   526	        } else {
   527	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   528	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   529	            return 1;
   530	        }
   531	    }
   532	
   533	    constexpr int max_grid_root = 46340;
   534	    if (n_elems_root <= 0 || n_elems_root > max_grid_root) {
   535	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Grid size must be between 1 and %d\n",
   536	                     max_grid_root);
   537	        return 1;
   538	    }
   539	    if (n_iters < 0) {
   540	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Iteration count must be non-negative\n");
   541	        return 1;
   542	    }
   543	    
   544	    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;
   545	    
   546	    printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
   547	    printf("============================================\n");
   548	    printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\n", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);
   549	    printf("Iterations: %d\n", n_iters);
   550	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   551	    printf("\n");
   552	    
   553	    // Build the unstructured mesh
   554	    printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
   555	    World world;
   556	    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);
   557	    
   558	    // Calculate memory usage
   559	    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);
   560	    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;
   561	    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;
   562	    printf("Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\n",
   563	           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   564	           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   565	           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
   566	    printf("\n");
   567	
   568	    // GPU allocation, layout conversion, host-to-device transfers, and graph
   569	    // construction are setup work and intentionally remain outside the timed
   570	    // simulation region.
   571	    GpuWorld gpu_world(world, n_iters);
   572	    
   573	    // Run simulation
   574	    printf("Running simulation...\n");
   575	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   576	    
   577	    gpu_world.run();
   578	    
   579	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   580	    const double duration_ms =
   581	        std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
   582	
   583	    // Preserve the host-visible result interface for hashing, validation, and
   584	    // optional external result reporting. This transfer is not computation.
   585	    gpu_world.download(world);
   586	    
   587	    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration_ms);
   588	    
   589	    // Calculate performance metrics
   590	    const double time_per_iter =
   591	        n_iters == 0 ? 0.0 : duration_ms / static_cast<double>(n_iters);
   592	    const double giga_elems_per_sec =
   593	        n_iters == 0
   594	            ? 0.0
   595	            : (static_cast<double>(n_iters) * n_elems) /
   596	                  (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;
   597	    
   598	    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)
   599	    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;
   600	    
   601	    printf("Performance:\n");
   602	    printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iter);
   603	    printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elems_per_sec);
   604	    printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   605	    
   606	    // Compute hash for verification
   607	    const uint64_t hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic);
   608	    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
   609	    printf("\n");
   610	    
   611	    // Print results for external validation
   612	    if (printResults) {
   613	        std::vector<double> energyData;
   614	        energyData.reserve(world.elements_dynamic.size());
   615	        for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
   616	            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);
   617	        }
   618	        print_results(energyData, "ElementEnergy");
   619	    }
   620	    
   621	    // Validation
   622	    if (validate) {
   623	        bool valid = validateResults(world);
   624	        if (!valid) {
   625	            return 1;
   626	        }
   627	    }
   628	    
   629	    return 0;
   630	}

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                \
+    do {                                                                      \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                    \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);   \
+        }                                                                     \
+    } while (false)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        allocate(count);
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    DeviceBuffer(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept
+        : data_(std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr)) {}
+
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept {
+        if (this != &other) {
+            if (data_ != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(data_);
+            }
+            data_ = std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr);
+        }
+        return *this;
+    }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                  count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

-// Compute energy flux between two elements
-inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
-                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
-    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
-           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
+// Materials are tiny and are read uniformly by almost every warp. Constant
+// memory avoids a global-memory indirection without specializing the mesh.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS];
+
+// Connectivity is stored slot-major on the device: all first neighbors are
+// contiguous, then all second neighbors, and so on. This preserves each
+// element's original neighbor order while making warp accesses coalesced.
+template <unsigned MaxDegree>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void simulationIteration(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_fluxes,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ current_energy,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ current_accumulated_flux,
+    val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+    val_t* __restrict__ next_accumulated_flux,
+    size_t element_count) {
+    const size_t element_idx =
+        static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (element_idx >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[element_idx]];
+    const val_t energy = current_energy[element_idx];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const unsigned connection_count = connection_counts[element_idx];
+
+    if constexpr (MaxDegree > 0) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned connection = 0; connection < MaxDegree; ++connection) {
+            if (connection < connection_count) {
+                const size_t connection_offset =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(connection) * element_count +
+                    element_idx;
+                const uint32_t neighbor_idx =
+                    connected_indices[connection_offset];
+                total_flux += (current_energy[neighbor_idx] - energy) *
+                              material.transfer_coeff *
+                              connected_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    next_energy[element_idx] = energy + total_flux;
+    next_accumulated_flux[element_idx] =
+        current_accumulated_flux[element_idx] + fabs(total_flux);
 }

-                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);
+class GpuWorld {
+public:
+    GpuWorld(const World& world, int n_iters)
+        : element_count_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        if (element_count_ > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr,
+                         "Mesh has too many elements for GPU connectivity\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        if (world.materials.empty() ||
+            world.materials.size() > MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "GPU supports between 1 and %d materials\n",
+                         MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS);
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }

+    GpuWorld(const GpuWorld&) = delete;
+    GpuWorld& operator=(const GpuWorld&) = delete;
+
+    void run() {
+        for (int batch = 0; batch < graph_batch_count_; ++batch) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(batch_graph_exec_, stream_));
+        }
+        if (remainder_graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(remainder_graph_exec_, stream_));
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world) const {
+        std::vector<val_t> host_energy(element_count_);
+        std::vector<val_t> host_accumulated_flux(element_count_);
+        const size_t byte_count = element_count_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_energy.data(), energy_[active_buffer_].get(),
+                              byte_count, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_accumulated_flux.data(),
+                              accumulated_flux_[active_buffer_].get(),
+                              byte_count, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < element_count_; ++i) {
+            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = host_energy[i];
+            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = host_accumulated_flux[i];
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <typename T>
+    static void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination,
+                             const std::vector<T>& source) {
+        if (!source.empty()) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(),
+                                  source.size() * sizeof(T),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        }
+    }
+
+    template <unsigned MaxDegree>
+    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
+        const unsigned write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1U;
+        const int block_count = static_cast<int>(
+            (element_count_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        simulationIteration<MaxDegree><<<block_count, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0,
+                                         stream_>>>(
+            material_indices_.get(), connection_counts_.get(),
+            connected_indices_.get(), connected_fluxes_.get(),
+            energy_[read_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[read_buffer].get(),
+            energy_[write_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[write_buffer].get(),
+            element_count_);
+    }
+
+    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
+        switch (max_degree_) {
+            case 0: launchIteration<0>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 1: launchIteration<1>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 2: launchIteration<2>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 3: launchIteration<3>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 4: launchIteration<4>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 5: launchIteration<5>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 6: launchIteration<6>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 7: launchIteration<7>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 8: launchIteration<8>(read_buffer); break;
+            default:
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported GPU mesh degree: %u\n",
+                             max_degree_);
+                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void prepareGraph(int n_iters) {
+        active_buffer_ = static_cast<unsigned>(n_iters) & 1U;
+        graph_batch_count_ = n_iters / CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE;
+        if (graph_batch_count_ != 0) {
+            batch_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
+        }
+        remainder_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(n_iters % CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
+    }
+
+    cudaGraphExec_t makeGraph(int iteration_count) {
+        if (iteration_count == 0) {
+            return nullptr;
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_,
+                                          cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iteration_count; ++iter) {
+            launchIteration(static_cast<unsigned>(iter) & 1U);
+        }
+
+        cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph));
+        cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+        return graph_exec;
+    }
+
+    size_t element_count_ = 0;
+    unsigned max_degree_ = 0;
+    unsigned active_buffer_ = 0;
+    int graph_batch_count_ = 0;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t batch_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t remainder_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> material_indices_;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> connection_counts_;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint32_t> connected_indices_;
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> connected_fluxes_;
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> energy_[2];
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> accumulated_flux_[2];
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                \
+    do {                                                                      \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                    \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);   \
+        }                                                                     \
+    } while (false)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        allocate(count);
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    DeviceBuffer(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept
+        : data_(std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr)) {}
+
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept {
+        if (this != &other) {
+            if (data_ != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(data_);
+            }
+            data_ = std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr);
+        }
+        return *this;
+    }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                  count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

-// Compute energy flux between two elements
-inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
-                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
-    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
-           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
+// Materials are tiny and are read uniformly by almost every warp. Constant
+// memory avoids a global-memory indirection without specializing the mesh.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS];
+
+// Connectivity is stored slot-major on the device: all first neighbors are
+// contiguous, then all second neighbors, and so on. This preserves each
+// element's original neighbor order while making warp accesses coalesced.
+template <unsigned MaxDegree>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void simulationIteration(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_fluxes,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ current_energy,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ current_accumulated_flux,
+    val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+    val_t* __restrict__ next_accumulated_flux,
+    size_t element_count) {
+    const size_t element_idx =
+        static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (element_idx >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[element_idx]];
+    const val_t energy = current_energy[element_idx];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const unsigned connection_count = connection_counts[element_idx];
+
+    if constexpr (MaxDegree > 0) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned connection = 0; connection < MaxDegree; ++connection) {
+            if (connection < connection_count) {
+                const size_t connection_offset =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(connection) * element_count +
+                    element_idx;
+                const uint32_t neighbor_idx =
+                    connected_indices[connection_offset];
+                total_flux += (current_energy[neighbor_idx] - energy) *
+                              material.transfer_coeff *
+                              connected_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    next_energy[element_idx] = energy + total_flux;
+    next_accumulated_flux[element_idx] =
+        current_accumulated_flux[element_idx] + fabs(total_flux);
 }

+    void run() {

+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(batch_graph_exec_, stream_));
+        }
+        if (remainder_graph_exec_ != nullptr) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(remainder_graph_exec_, stream_));
+        }

+    void download(World& world) const {
+        std::vector<val_t> host_energy(element_count_);
+        std::vector<val_t> host_accumulated_flux(element_count_);
+        const size_t byte_count = element_count_ * sizeof(val_t);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_energy.data(), energy_[active_buffer_].get(),
+                              byte_count, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_accumulated_flux.data(),
+                              accumulated_flux_[active_buffer_].get(),
+                              byte_count, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < element_count_; ++i) {
+            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = host_energy[i];
+            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = host_accumulated_flux[i];
+        }
+    }

+    template <typename T>
+    static void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination,
+                             const std::vector<T>& source) {
+        if (!source.empty()) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination.get(), source.data(),
+                                  source.size() * sizeof(T),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        }
+    }

+    template <unsigned MaxDegree>
+    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
+        const unsigned write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1U;
+        const int block_count = static_cast<int>(
+            (element_count_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        simulationIteration<MaxDegree><<<block_count, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0,
+                                         stream_>>>(
+            material_indices_.get(), connection_counts_.get(),
+            connected_indices_.get(), connected_fluxes_.get(),
+            energy_[read_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[read_buffer].get(),
+            energy_[write_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[write_buffer].get(),
+            element_count_);
+    }
+
+    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
+        switch (max_degree_) {
+            case 0: launchIteration<0>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 1: launchIteration<1>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 2: launchIteration<2>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 3: launchIteration<3>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 4: launchIteration<4>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 5: launchIteration<5>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 6: launchIteration<6>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 7: launchIteration<7>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 8: launchIteration<8>(read_buffer); break;
+            default:
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported GPU mesh degree: %u\n",
+                             max_degree_);
+                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void prepareGraph(int n_iters) {
+        active_buffer_ = static_cast<unsigned>(n_iters) & 1U;
+        graph_batch_count_ = n_iters / CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE;
+        if (graph_batch_count_ != 0) {
+            batch_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
+        }
+        remainder_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(n_iters % CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
+    }
+
+    cudaGraphExec_t makeGraph(int iteration_count) {
+        if (iteration_count == 0) {
+            return nullptr;
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_,
+                                          cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iteration_count; ++iter) {
+            launchIteration(static_cast<unsigned>(iter) & 1U);
+        }
+
+        cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph));
+        cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+        return graph_exec;
+    }
+
+    size_t element_count_ = 0;
+    unsigned max_degree_ = 0;
+    unsigned active_buffer_ = 0;
+    int graph_batch_count_ = 0;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t batch_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t remainder_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> material_indices_;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> connection_counts_;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint32_t> connected_indices_;
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> connected_fluxes_;
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> energy_[2];
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> accumulated_flux_[2];
+};

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                \
+    do {                                                                      \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                    \
+        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                \
+            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);   \
+        }                                                                     \
+    } while (false)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    DeviceBuffer() = default;
+
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        allocate(count);
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    DeviceBuffer(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept
+        : data_(std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr)) {}
+
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(DeviceBuffer&& other) noexcept {
+        if (this != &other) {
+            if (data_ != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(data_);
+            }
+            data_ = std::exchange(other.data_, nullptr);
+        }
+        return *this;
+    }
+
+    void allocate(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                  count * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    T* get() { return data_; }
+    const T* get() const { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

-// Compute energy flux between two elements

+// Materials are tiny and are read uniformly by almost every warp. Constant
+// memory avoids a global-memory indirection without specializing the mesh.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[MAX_DEVICE_MATERIALS];
+
+// Connectivity is stored slot-major on the device: all first neighbors are
+// contiguous, then all second neighbors, and so on. This preserves each
+// element's original neighbor order while making warp accesses coalesced.
+template <unsigned MaxDegree>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE)
+void simulationIteration(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_fluxes,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ current_energy,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ current_accumulated_flux,
+    val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
+    val_t* __restrict__ next_accumulated_flux,
+    size_t element_count) {
+    const size_t element_idx =
+        static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (element_idx >= element_count) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[element_idx]];
+    const val_t energy = current_energy[element_idx];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const unsigned connection_count = connection_counts[element_idx];
+
+    if constexpr (MaxDegree > 0) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned connection = 0; connection < MaxDegree; ++connection) {
+            if (connection < connection_count) {
+                const size_t connection_offset =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(connection) * element_count +
+                    element_idx;
+                const uint32_t neighbor_idx =
+                    connected_indices[connection_offset];
+                total_flux += (current_energy[neighbor_idx] - energy) *
+                              material.transfer_coeff *
+                              connected_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    next_energy[element_idx] = energy + total_flux;
+    next_accumulated_flux[element_idx] =
+        current_accumulated_flux[element_idx] + fabs(total_flux);
 }

+    GpuWorld(const GpuWorld&) = delete;
+    GpuWorld& operator=(const GpuWorld&) = delete;

+    void run() {

+    void download(World& world) const {

+    template <typename T>
+    static void copyToDevice(DeviceBuffer<T>& destination,

+    template <unsigned MaxDegree>
+    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
+        const unsigned write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1U;
+        const int block_count = static_cast<int>(
+            (element_count_ + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        simulationIteration<MaxDegree><<<block_count, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0,
+                                         stream_>>>(
+            material_indices_.get(), connection_counts_.get(),
+            connected_indices_.get(), connected_fluxes_.get(),
+            energy_[read_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[read_buffer].get(),
+            energy_[write_buffer].get(), accumulated_flux_[write_buffer].get(),
+            element_count_);
+    }
+
+    void launchIteration(unsigned read_buffer) {
+        switch (max_degree_) {
+            case 0: launchIteration<0>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 1: launchIteration<1>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 2: launchIteration<2>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 3: launchIteration<3>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 4: launchIteration<4>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 5: launchIteration<5>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 6: launchIteration<6>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 7: launchIteration<7>(read_buffer); break;
+            case 8: launchIteration<8>(read_buffer); break;
+            default:
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported GPU mesh degree: %u\n",
+                             max_degree_);
+                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+    }
+
+    void prepareGraph(int n_iters) {
+        active_buffer_ = static_cast<unsigned>(n_iters) & 1U;
+        graph_batch_count_ = n_iters / CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE;
+        if (graph_batch_count_ != 0) {
+            batch_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
+        }
+        remainder_graph_exec_ = makeGraph(n_iters % CUDA_GRAPH_BATCH_SIZE);
+    }
+
+    cudaGraphExec_t makeGraph(int iteration_count) {
+        if (iteration_count == 0) {
+            return nullptr;
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_,
+                                          cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iteration_count; ++iter) {
+            launchIteration(static_cast<unsigned>(iter) & 1U);
+        }
+
+        cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph));
+        cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_exec, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+        return graph_exec;
+    }
+
+    size_t element_count_ = 0;
+    unsigned max_degree_ = 0;
+    unsigned active_buffer_ = 0;
+    int graph_batch_count_ = 0;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t batch_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t remainder_graph_exec_ = nullptr;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> material_indices_;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint8_t> connection_counts_;
+    DeviceBuffer<uint32_t> connected_indices_;
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> connected_fluxes_;
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> energy_[2];
+    DeviceBuffer<val_t> accumulated_flux_[2];
+};
+

     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
